# germany-einvoice: figure and rule ledger

Guide: [germany-einvoice](../../skills/germany/germany-einvoice.md). Accepted Guide SHA-256: `d2017d1ab2cce52ae1d416036f9d18f331b35f1aa7e9221ab24307d126b3bddb`.
Repackaged from the completed [independent country review](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/blob/bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf/ledgers/germany-2026-evidence/independent-review/report.md) at `bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf`. The Guide bytes, all current ledger claims and all original-topic destinations are preserved. This is a packaging change; it does not claim a new legal review or accountant attestation.
Original published ledger SHA-256: `d208d8d2e296186a2d7cb06a9c5ec0f8926e3de1e6419b7b5f52bd913f4fef18`. Full source captures, earlier versions, scripts and review chains remain in the original archive; none is added by this replacement. Source excerpts below retain their actual read dates. Short excerpts identify the previously reviewed operative unit; they do not replace its complete conditions.

| ID | Section / native LF | Exact current wording | Source anchors / disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| 001 | E-invoicing in Germany (E-Rechnung, XRechnung and ZUGFeRD) LF[21, 21] | When a German business must issue an e-invoice, when a paper or PDF invoice is still allowed, what every business must be able to receive, which formats count, how to correct, check and store e-invoices, and how invoicing federal authorities differs. It is for businesses, freelancers, landlords and associations in Germany, for foreign businesses that invoice German customers, and for the people who keep their books. Figures are for tax year 2026. The rules are read from the consolidated federal law pages (UStG, UStDV, AO, ERechV), from the finance ministry's letter of 15 October 2025, which writes the e-invoice rules into the VAT application decree (Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass), from the ministry's questions and answers page (as of March 2026), and from the GoBD change letters of 11 March 2024 and 14 July 2025. The reduced VAT rate is printed as a digit only in the ministry's tax booklet, 2025 edition; the statute states it in words. The ministry's first e-invoice letter, of 15 October 2024, is no longer at its address on the ministry's site, so nothing in this Guide rests on it alone. | Preserved scope/routing/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. | 033 | Who Must Comply LF[67, 67] | - **Duty to invoice.** For a supply to another business for its business, the supplier must issue an invoice "innerhalb von sechs Monaten nach Ausführung der Leistung" (within six months after the supply), unless the supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 034 | Who Must Comply LF[68, 68] | - **Duty to use an e-invoice.** That invoice must be an e-invoice when supplier and customer are both established in Germany (Inland) or in one of the areas named in § 1(3) UStG. | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 035 | Who Must Comply LF[69, 69] | - **Established** means: seat, place of management, a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply, or, with no seat, the home or usual place of stay (§ 14(2) sentence 3 UStG). A foreign business that is only registered for VAT in Germany, with no fixed establishment here, may say so on its invoice to explain why it sends no e-invoice; the recipient may rely on that with a prudent merchant's care (ministry questions and answers, no. 3). | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7), [EI.FAQSCOPE](#q-cc04f2dc2f534e). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

| 077 | Receiving e-invoices LF[134, 134] | - **Apply the consent table.** No consent is needed for the domestic e-invoice duty, or the stated domestic business-to-business § 33/34/34a UStDV cases. Remaining voluntary cases, including consumers, the stated exempt supplies and a supplier or customer abroad, require the recipient's consent. | [EI.RECEIVE](#q-48e84d41ccfcc5), [R39.default-and-consent](#q-45a4e1e5323ee3), [R40.consent](#q-9d46fec31872ce). R37–43 bounded source-backed condition successor |
| 078 | Receiving e-invoices LF[135, 135] | - **E-mail.** E-invoices may come by e-mail. The letter says: "Bei einem Empfang mittels E-Mail ist kein gesondertes E-Mail-Postfach nur für den Empfang von E-Rechnungen erforderlich." The ministry's questions and answers page goes one step further and says an e-mail inbox is already enough to receive (nos. 8 and 12). | [EI.RECEIVE](#q-48e84d41ccfcc5). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 079 | Receiving e-invoices LF[136, 136] | - **Refusing does not help.** A business that cannot receive an e-invoice, or refuses it, has no right to an other invoice instead. The issuer's VAT duties then count as met if it issued an e-invoice and can show that it tried to send it properly, for example with a send log. | [EI.FAQCHANNEL](#q-4ee85f53488936). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 080 | Receiving e-invoices LF[137, 137] | - **Receiving is not processing.** The recipient need not process the data electronically beyond what storage demands (questions and answers, no. 9). | [EI.VIEWER](#q-a34e44374c3508). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 081 | Receiving e-invoices LF[138, 138] | - **Reading the file.** An XRechnung has no picture part; a viewer is needed. The tax administration offers one for XRechnung and ZUGFeRD files at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung and calls it a non-binding service. | [EI.STRUCTURED](#q-83a3453a5636f5), [EI.INPUT](#q-56b9f6c3a8d0cd). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 082 | Receiving e-invoices LF[139, 139] | - **Associations.** An association must be able to receive e-invoices so far as it acts as a business. For supplies to its non-business side it need neither receive nor issue e-invoices (questions and answers, no. 5). | [EI.FAQSCOPE](#q-cc04f2dc2f534e). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

| 096 | Transitional Rules (2025-2027) LF[164, 164] | - **Two dates and the default duty: selected combined-law interpretation.** Each § 27(38) relief tests both the supply date and the last transmission date. For an ordinary taxable domestic business-to-business supply made in December 2026, first invoiced in January 2027, the default § 14(2) e-invoice duty applies if both parties meet the domestic-establishment test, the recipient acquires for its business, the invoice total is above EUR 250, and no Kleinunternehmer, ticket or other permanent exemption applies. This fixture assumes no previously issued qualifying advance or standing invoice; those follow their specific rules below. No. 1 relief ended on 31 December 2026; nos. 2 and 3 concern supplies made after that date, so neither low issuer turnover nor the December supply date rescues this January invoice. This is a labelled reading of the own default and transition clauses, not a separate ruling. The same December supply first sent by 31 December 2026 can use paper or an agreed PDF under no. 1. A supply made on 1 January 2027 instead enters no. 2's own prior-year-turnover test and 2027 transmission window. The ministry applies the principles to post-2024 supplies, subject to the actual transition. Reapply these exact duty/relief gates to other late invoices rather than inferring a gap in all invoicing rules. | [EI.TRANSITION](#q-42603ba2a8946d), [R39.default-and-consent](#q-45a4e1e5323ee3), [R39.transition](#q-0d5e5a2ccab88d), [R39.effect](#q-1cd68ff2514f45). R37–43 bounded source-backed condition successor |

| 122 | Accepted Formats LF[201, 201] | - **Hybrid files: the XML leads.** Before 2025 the picture part led. Now the structured part is the leading part, and if the two differ the structured data decide (decree section 14.4(3); questions and answers, no. 12a). Input tax can be deducted only from the structured part. A picture part with different invoice details can count as a further (other) invoice, for which § 14c UStG must be checked: that section makes a person owe tax that is shown wrongly or without the right to show it (decree section 14c.1(4a)). Small technical differences, such as a shortened description or rounding differences, are not objected to. | [EI.HYBRID](#q-f5c551592fbc87). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 123 | Accepted Formats LF[202, 202] | - **Everything the VAT law demands must be in the structured part.** All details under §§ 14 and 14a UStG belong there. A mere reference in the data to an attachment that holds them in unstructured form is not enough. A link to an outside target is not enough. Extra detail, such as a timesheet as a PDF, may sit in an attachment inside the e-invoice (decree section 14.5(1) and (15)). | [EI.STRUCTURED](#q-83a3453a5636f5). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

The channels below are from the legacy Guide and from the federal e-invoicing information site named in the ministry's questions and answers page (e-rechnung-bund.de, run by the procurement office of the interior ministry). Its current upload and e-mail instructions and its September 2025 consolidation statement were read on 29 October 2026 and are linked in Sources. No user account was opened or invoice submitted. | [EI.PORTAL](#q-997959acc4752e), [EI.FEDCHANNELS](#q-e77c044d145b5a), [EI.CONSOLIDATION](#q-740bddfd92d39c). | 233 | Rounding LF[388, 388] | These points describe the data format. The amount datatype and line-net formula are checked against XRechnung 3.0.2 sections 8.1 and 12.5 (PEPPOL-EN16931-R120); the tax-group rule is BR-CO-17. The specification of the format in use decides, and no rounding mode is inferred. | [EI.R120](#q-2c993482274b48), [EI.AMOUNT](#q-f34453a7ed0d30), [EI.ROUND](#q-7e63a462bed586). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 234 | Rounding LF[390, 390] | - **Line net amount (BT-131):** quantity × (item net price / price-base quantity), plus line charges, less line allowances (PEPPOL-EN16931-R120). Amounts are expressed with two decimal places (section 8.1). Quantity × unit price alone describes only the case with a price-base quantity of one and no line charges or allowances. | [EI.R120](#q-2c993482274b48), [EI.AMOUNT](#q-f34453a7ed0d30), [EI.LINENET](#q-302c8d3d160d7c). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 235 | Rounding LF[391, 391] | - **Tax amount rounding:** the format has no tax amount per line. The tax is worked out once per VAT breakdown group (BG-23), that is per tax category and rate: the group's taxable amount (BT-116) times its rate (BT-119), divided by 100, rounded to two decimal places (rule BR-CO-17). The legacy Guide's tax amount per line was wrong | [EI.ROUND](#q-7e63a462bed586). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

| 261 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[430, 430] | \| Retention period \| 8 years for a copy of every invoice issued and for every invoice received (§ 14b(1) UStG). The period starts at the end of the calendar year in which the invoice was issued. § 147(3) AO stays untouched: under it the period does not end while the documents still matter for a tax whose assessment period is open. Start of the eight-year period: it applies to every invoice whose old retention period had not yet run out on 31 December 2024; for banks, insurers and securities institutions only to invoices whose old period had not run out on 1 January 2026 (§ 27(40) UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html) \| | [EI.KEEP](#q-2911f6621d38b8), [EI.TRANSITION](#q-42603ba2a8946d), [EI.AOBOOK](#q-157649f511c397). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

The human-readable part must be kept only if it holds extra or different information that matters for tax, for example booking notes or qualified electronic signatures (GoBD paragraphs 119 and 131). The XML must not be lost through a format change, for example to TIFF (GoBD paragraph 125) \| | [EI.GOBDOUT](#q-99b2a813062b2a). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 266 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[435, 435] | \| Outgoing invoices \| GoBD 76 permits omitting the outgoing image/PDF copy only under the preceding full-content/highest-machine-evaluability conditions and if an identical copy can be produced on request at any time. The actually processed format must carry the full content with the highest machine evaluability; otherwise keep both formats. Among the additional requirements, continuously historise master data and terms; document which terms and original format applied; retain the original layout as a template and historise changes; keep invoicing data machine-evaluable and immutable. Reproducing a current-looking image while overwriting historical customer/terms/layout data does not establish the waiver. This does not remove the separate structured-original/XML duties. \| | [EI.GOBDOUT](#q-99b2a813062b2a), [R42.outgoing-waiver](#q-ef95fb79056deb), [R43.effective](#q-389d2d377b4d7a). | 269 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[438, 438] | \| Verfahrensdokumentation \| GoBD 150–155 requires clear, current process documentation allowing a knowledgeable third party to understand and check the system within reasonable time; its extent depends on the actual business/system complexity. Describe creation, indexing, processing, storage, retrieval, machine evaluation, security and reproduction. Documentation must correspond to the programme versions actually used, with traceable dated/version history retained as long as needed to understand the records. Under paragraph 155, missing or inadequate documentation is not a substantive formal defect justifying rejection of the books where traceability and verifiability are not impaired; no automatic penalty or universal certification follows. \| | [R43.documentation](#q-40af79cfd3f767), [R43.effective](#q-389d2d377b4d7a). | 270 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[439, 439] | \| Storage location \| In Germany. Outside the EU only under § 146(2b) AO, which needs the tax office's approval on application (§ 14b(5) UStG) \| | [EI.KEEP](#q-2911f6621d38b8), [EI.AOLOCATION](#q-d200ca9b9a7285). | 271 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[440, 440] | \| Audit access \| Three forms under § 147(6) AO: the tax office inspects the stored data and uses the system itself (Z1, direct access); it demands that the data be evaluated by machine as it specifies (Z2, indirect access); or it demands that the data be handed over in a machine-readable format (Z3, data handover, for example on a data carrier or through a data exchange platform). The legacy Guide still called Z3 a data carrier handover; the GoBD change of 11 March 2024 renamed it \| | [EI.AOBOOK](#q-157649f511c397), [EI.GOBDZ2](#q-a9c6734358d0b9), [EI.GOBD2024](#q-794eaae8b04cb9). | 272 | Section 8: Archiving Requirements LF[441, 441] | \| Conversion \| Under GoBD 135, the default for in-house conversion is to retain both versions, assign them to the same record/index and mark the converted one. Converted-only retention is permitted only if no image/content changes, no other retention-required information is lost, proper lossless conversion is documented, and machine evaluability/tax-authority access is unrestricted. This general exception does not permit losing mandatory original structured invoice data or the XML barred from loss by GoBD 125; keep the independent VAT structured-original rule above. Actual conversion and compliance require records, not a software-brand assumption. \| | [R43.conversion](#q-55c7e211c23071), [R43.effective](#q-389d2d377b4d7a). | 280 | Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance LF[455, 455] | \| Other invoice where an e-invoice was due: input VAT deduction \| The invoice is not proper under §§ 14/14a, so it gives no deduction in principle (UStAE 15.2a(1)). A later e-invoice referring specifically and clearly to it supplies the ordinary cure (15.2a(7)). Without cure, the full 15.2a(1a) strict-evidence route requires an actual invoice, although formally faulty, and objective evidence allowing easy, unambiguous verification of every material condition: actual goods/services supplied by another entrepreneur for the claimant's taxable outputs and VAT actually paid. An invoice or copy openly showing VAT is necessary to prove payment of that tax; an invoice cannot be dispensed with entirely. Establish supply timing rather than automatically assuming it equals the invoice date where there are doubts. The authority need not find missing information itself; doubts and gaps fall on the claimant. Sentences 12–14 expressly apply this to wrong-format invoices and say an otherwise correct, complete invoice regularly meets the conditions. Stipulated ordinary domestic taxable supply above the small-invoice limit: a complete PDF showing EUR 1,000 net/EUR 190 legally due VAT, actual business supply and EUR 1,190 payment, all otherwise eligible under § 15 with objective records, can support EUR 190 input despite the wrong format. A missing invoice/open VAT, unproved supply/payment or other failed material gate is not presumed to support that deduction. Contested evidence requires actual resolution; neither any-PDF credit nor all-PDF denial is the method. \| | [EI.INPUT](#q-56b9f6c3a8d0cd), [EI.CURE](#q-98061b72b18821), [EI.EXCEPTIONINPUT](#q-9c5f36f2cfb911), [R41.amendment-input](#q-1fe9b25cd08eae), [R41.amendment-fallback](#q-156acf6da527c1), [R41.full-fallback-conditions](#q-72e056b6f25632), [R41.full-fallback-completion](#q-d7579c243117fc). R37–43 bounded source-backed condition successor |

| 298 | Income Tax Integration LF[486, 486] | - For Einzelunternehmer (sole proprietors) and Freiberufler (freelancers), e-invoice revenue data feeds into the Einkommensteuer return (Anlage S or Anlage G) | Preserved scope/routing/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 299 | Income Tax Integration LF[487, 487] | - **Correction.** Under the EÜR (Einnahmenüberschussrechnung, cash basis, § 4(3) EStG) income counts in the calendar year in which it flows in (§ 11(1) EStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html), not at the invoice date. The legacy Guide's advice to align e-invoice dates with payment dates was wrong: an invoice is dated when it is issued, and must be issued within six months of the supply | [EI.CASH](#q-ee828cafae1797), [EI.CASHTIME](#q-f4d709627c2442), [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 300 | Income Tax Integration LF[488, 488] | - **Correction.** Under Bilanzierung (accruals basis) income is booked whatever the payment date, and profit counts when it is realised (§ 252(1) nos. 4 and 5 HGB, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__252.html). The invoice date does not decide. See the bookkeeping Guide, `germany-bookkeeping` | [EI.ACCRUAL](#q-4cb47eebc407b7). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 301 | Intra-EU Reporting LF[492, 492] | - An invoice to a business customer in another EU country is outside the German e-invoice duty, because the customer is not established in Germany. It may be an e-invoice if the customer agrees. The rules of § 14a UStG on content and the shorter deadline still apply | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7), [EI.NEXT15](#q-f854e79e6b0bf9). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 302 | Intra-EU Reporting LF[493, 493] | - Intra-EU supplies reported via Zusammenfassende Meldung (recapitulative statement) must reconcile with the invoices issued for them (tax category K in the structured data) | Preserved scope/routing/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 303 | Intra-EU Reporting LF[494, 494] | - Buyer VAT IDs on invoices (BT-48) should be checked against VIES | Preserved scope/routing/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 304 | Intra-EU Reporting LF[495, 495] | - Exempt supplies under § 4 nos. 1 to 7 UStG between two German businesses, for example an intra-Community supply from Germany to another German business's fixed establishment elsewhere in the EU, DO need an e-invoice (decree section 14.1(4)) | [EI.BMFEST](#q-c515f7df52be8b). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 305 | GoBD and Tax Audit LF[499, 499] | - Tax auditors may use Z1 (direct access), Z2 (machine evaluation by the taxpayer as the office specifies) or Z3 (data handover) for archived e-invoices: see Section 8 | [EI.AOBOOK](#q-157649f511c397), [EI.GOBDZ2](#q-a9c6734358d0b9), [EI.GOBD2024](#q-794eaae8b04cb9). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 306 | GoBD and Tax Audit LF[500, 500] | - Verfahrensdokumentation follows the actual GoBD 150–155 process/version-history method and paragraph 155 qualification in Section 8. The documentation and archive/implementation records must describe the actual workflow; no universal software certification is asserted. | [R43.documentation](#q-40af79cfd3f767), [R43.effective](#q-389d2d377b4d7a). R37–43 bounded source-backed condition successor |
| 307 | GoBD and Tax Audit LF[501, 501] | - If the archive cannot be produced in an audit, the tax office can estimate (§ 162 AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html), and a delay payment can be set when data access is not granted: see Section 9 | [EI.ESTIMATE](#q-b6ccbc47720d74), [EI.AOLOCATION](#q-d200ca9b9a7285). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 308 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[505, 505] | All from the letter of 15 October 2025 (decree sections in brackets) at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 or from the questions and answers page at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/FAQ/e-rechnung.html | Preserved scope/routing/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 309 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[507, 507] | - **Self-billing (Gutschrift).** The e-invoice rules apply in the same way when the customer issues the invoice. If a self-billing document and an ordinary invoice cannot be combined in one e-invoice for technical reasons, separate e-invoices are issued (14.3(1) and (2)). | [EI.SELFBILL](#q-be0534d1e47480), [EI.SEPARATE](#q-187c164677e29e). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 310 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[508, 508] | - **Special schemes.** The duty also covers supplies under the special rules of §§ 23a to 25c UStG (for example travel services and the margin scheme) and reverse-charge supplies under § 13b UStG between German businesses (14.1(4)). | [EI.SPECIAL](#q-5156cf3bec58e2). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 311 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[509, 509] | - **Opted supplies.** A supply that is taxable only because of an option under § 9(1) UStG needs an e-invoice (14.1(4)). | [EI.SPECIAL](#q-5156cf3bec58e2). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 312 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[510, 510] | - **Rent and other continuing contracts.** One e-invoice for the first part period is enough, if the contract is attached to it or the e-invoice otherwise makes clear that it is a standing invoice. When an invoice detail changes, for example a rent increase, a new e-invoice is due. A standing invoice that was properly issued as an other invoice needs no extra e-invoice as long as its details do not change (14.1(19), 14.5(17)). The invoice number may be a number from the contract, such as a flat or tenant number (14.5(12)). | [EI.RENT](#q-b2bd08818bbe76), [EI.RENTCHANGE](#q-0c5686f0817c8a), [EI.NUMBER](#q-a35bdd2bba0f47). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 313 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[511, 511] | - **Contracts as invoices.** A contract with all invoice details is an other invoice unless it meets the format rules. Where an e-invoice is due, an e-invoice must be issued in addition; the contract can sit in its attachment (14.1(17)). | [EI.CONTRACT](#q-23731051161a0b). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 314 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[512, 512] | - **Bank statements** that bill the bank's own service are invoices. They are e-invoices only if they meet the format and content rules (14.1(16)). | [EI.BANK](#q-09c66a0ab43832). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 315 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[513, 513] | - **Inside one business.** Documents for movements between departments of the same business, or inside a VAT group (Organkreis), are internal vouchers, not invoices (14.1(1)). The e-invoice duty does not reach them. | [EI.INTERNAL](#q-132ec346261f12). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 316 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[514, 514] | - **Customer with a business and a private side.** For a legal person that buys for both sides, the e-invoice duty takes priority (14.1(8)). Where several persons buy jointly and businesses are among them, the invoice to that community must be an e-invoice once no transition relief applies (15.2a(3)). | [EI.MIXED](#q-992c64cdc865d7), [EI.COMMUNITY](#q-d89e2dcef92a45). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 317 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[515, 515] | - **Corrections.** Where an e-invoice was due, the correction must also be an e-invoice, using the invoice type for corrections. Sending the missing or wrong detail in another form is not enough (14.11(1)). Single line items may be corrected in a later e-invoice if it refers specifically and clearly to the first one (questions and answers, no. 7b). | [EI.CORRECT](#q-8063f5f55f5540), [EI.FAQBUILD](#q-9856778e43eaf1). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 318 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[516, 516] | - **Price changes are not corrections.** Cash discounts, rebates and bonuses paid later, and price cuts after a complaint that leave the supply itself unchanged, change the tax base under § 17 UStG; the invoice need not be corrected. A change in what was supplied, such as changed quantities on a building invoice, does need a correction, at least of the description (14.11(4), 17.1(3a)). | [EI.PRICE](#q-cb3f5566ea9436), [EI.PRICELATER](#q-dd874227a8b1bd), [EI.PRICE2](#q-2f981edc373300). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 319 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[517, 517] | - **Building work.** For now it is enough if the structured part lists the single trades with their sums, and a human-readable attachment, clearly referred to in the structured part, gives the detailed breakdown (questions and answers, no. 7b). | [EI.FAQBUILD](#q-9856778e43eaf1). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 320 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[518, 518] | - **Final invoices.** Part payments already received may be deducted in an unstructured attachment, if the structured part points to it expressly. The ministry says this can also be used after 31 December 2027 (questions and answers, no. 7b). | [EI.FAQBUILD](#q-9856778e43eaf1). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 321 | Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled LF[519, 519] | - **Fee notices of public bodies** that count as invoices follow the general rules, e-invoice duty included; one issued as an other invoice can be corrected by an e-invoice later (14.1(1)). | [EI.INTERNAL](#q-132ec346261f12). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 322 | The method, step by step LF[523, 523] | 1. Check whether an invoice is due at all. § 14(2) sentence 2 UStG demands one, within six months, for supplies to another business for its business, to a legal person that is not a business, and for taxable property-related work for anyone else. No duty for supplies exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 323 | The method, step by step LF[524, 524] | 2. Check whether it must be an e-invoice. Yes, if supplier and customer are both established in Germany and the customer buys for its business (§ 14(2) sentence 2 no. 1 UStG). No, if one of them is established abroad, or the customer is a consumer or a legal person that is not a business. Same page as step 1. | [EI.DUTY](#q-5cde48cc05f7b7), [EI.FAQSCOPE](#q-cc04f2dc2f534e). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 324 | The method, step by step LF[525, 525] | 3. Check the lasting exceptions: small invoices (§ 33 UStDV), passenger tickets (§ 34 UStDV), invoices of a Kleinunternehmer (§ 34a UStDV). These may always be other invoices. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__33.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34a.html | [EI.SMALL250](#q-f9b4ea39f2d1a9), [EI.TICKET](#q-62cb86084b2d75), [EI.KUFIELDS](#q-46950d60e08c5f). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 325 | The method, step by step LF[526, 526] | 4. Check the transition relief in § 27(38) UStG: the year of the supply, the day the invoice is sent and, for 2027 sales, the issuer's total turnover of the previous calendar year. If the relief applies and the issuer wants to use it: paper, or with the recipient's consent another electronic format. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html | [EI.TRANSITION](#q-42603ba2a8946d). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 326 | The method, step by step LF[527, 527] | 5. If an e-invoice is due or wanted, pick an allowed format: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD from version 2.0.1 without MINIMUM and BASIC-WL, another format that meets the European standard, or an agreed format that allows full extraction. Put every detail of § 14(4) and § 14a UStG into the structured part. Ministry letter of 15 October 2025: https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | [EI.STANDARD](#q-d7e99a8944e0f5), [EI.ZUGFERD](#q-4486126633a773), [EI.EDI](#q-306556a71b14bb), [EI.STRUCTURED](#q-83a3453a5636f5). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 327 | The method, step by step LF[528, 528] | 6. Validate the file, keep the validation report, and send it by a channel agreed with the customer. Apply the consent table before transmission: no consent for the domestic duty or its stated domestic § 33/34/34a cases; obtain consent for the remaining voluntary cases. Keep proof of sending. Same letter as step 5. | [EI.VALIDATE](#q-544cdbce8340ac), [EI.RECEIVE](#q-48e84d41ccfcc5), [EI.FAQCHANNEL](#q-4ee85f53488936), [R39.default-and-consent](#q-45a4e1e5323ee3), [R40.consent](#q-9d46fec31872ce). R37–43 bounded source-backed condition successor |
| 328 | The method, step by step LF[529, 529] | 7. On the receiving side: make sure e-invoices can be received, display the XML with a viewer (the tax administration offers one at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung ), and check the invoice details before deducting input tax under § 15 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__15.html | [EI.RECEIVE](#q-48e84d41ccfcc5), [EI.VIEWER](#q-a34e44374c3508), [EI.INPUT](#q-56b9f6c3a8d0cd). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 329 | The method, step by step LF[530, 530] | 8. Correct a faulty invoice under § 31(5) UStDV with a document that refers specifically and clearly to it. Where an e-invoice was due, the correction is an e-invoice too. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__31.html | [EI.MONTH](#q-03d3f8c3d715b5), [EI.CORRECT](#q-8063f5f55f5540). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 330 | The method, step by step LF[531, 531] | 9. Store the structured part unchanged, in the format received, for the period in § 14b(1) UStG, and keep it machine-readable for the tax office. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html | [EI.KEEP](#q-2911f6621d38b8), [EI.ORIGINAL](#q-ac05febac47258). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |
| 331 | The method, step by step LF[532, 532] | 10. For a federal authority, follow the ERechV as well: XRechnung or an equal standard, Leitweg-ID, sign-up at the federal portal. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html | [EI.PORTAL](#q-997959acc4752e), [EI.FEDFIELDS](#q-f87affff7b651b). Exact prior current unit with rebound native coordinates |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF21 | When a German business must issue an e-invoice, when a paper or PDF invoice is still allowed, what every business must be able to receive, which formats count, how to correct, check and store e-invoices, and how invoicing federal authorities differs. It is for businesses, freelancers, landlords and associations in Germany, for foreign businesses that invoice German customers, and for the people who keep their books. Figures are for tax year 2026. The rules are read from the consolidated federal law pages (UStG, UStDV, AO, ERechV), from the finance ministry's letter of 15 October 2025, which writes the e-invoice rules into the VAT application decree (Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass), from the ministry's questions and answers page (as of March 2026), and from the GoBD change letters of 11 March 2024 and 14 July 2025. The reduced VAT rate is printed as a digit only in the ministry's tax booklet, 2025 edition; the statute states it in words. The ministry's first e-invoice letter, of 15 October 2024, is no longer at its address on the ministry's site, so nothing in this Guide rests on it alone. | Exact retained currentLF21, E-invoicing in Germany (E-Rechnung, XRechnung and ZUGFeRD)  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF46 | - **The duty to RECEIVE has no exceptions.** A Kleinunternehmer, a landlord of flats, a doctor and an association with a business side must all be able to receive e-invoices since 1 January 2025. Kleinunternehmer are released from ISSUING only. | Exact retained currentLF46, Section 1: Quick Reference  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF47 | - **Paper needs no consent.** During the transition a paper invoice may always be sent. Only an invoice in another electronic format, for example a plain PDF by e-mail, needs the recipient's consent. | Exact retained currentLF47, Section 1: Quick Reference R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF48 | - **An e-invoice to a German business needs no consent.** The recipient cannot insist on paper or PDF. | Exact retained currentLF48, Section 1: Quick Reference R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF49 | - **A plain PDF is not an e-invoice.** A hybrid ZUGFeRD file, a PDF that carries the structured data, can be one. | Exact retained currentLF49, Section 1: Quick Reference  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF50 | - **The turnover limit is a test of the ISSUER, for sales made in 2027 only.** It is not a phase that starts a duty. The duty exists since 2025; the limit decides who may still use the relief in 2027. | Exact retained currentLF50, Section 1: Quick Reference  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF67 | - **Duty to invoice.** For a supply to another business for its business, the supplier must issue an invoice "innerhalb von sechs Monaten nach Ausführung der Leistung" (within six months after the supply), unless the supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. | Exact retained currentLF67, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF68 | - **Duty to use an e-invoice.** That invoice must be an e-invoice when supplier and customer are both established in Germany (Inland) or in one of the areas named in § 1(3) UStG. | Exact retained currentLF68, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF69 | - **Established** means: seat, place of management, a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply, or, with no seat, the home or usual place of stay (§ 14(2) sentence 3 UStG). A foreign business that is only registered for VAT in Germany, with no fixed establishment here, may say so on its invoice to explain why it sends no e-invoice; the recipient may rely on that with a prudent merchant's care (ministry questions and answers, no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF69, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF70 | - **Business** is wide. The ministry's questions and answers page counts freelancers and people with exempt sales only, such as landlords of flats, Kleinunternehmer and doctors (no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF70, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF71 | - **Shorter deadlines stay.** § 14a UStG sets the fifteenth day of the following month for intra-Community supplies and for services under § 3a(2) UStG performed in another member state. See https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14a.html | Exact retained currentLF71, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF72 | - **Advance payments.** If money is received before the supply, the six months run from the receipt (decree section 14.1(3)). | Exact retained currentLF72, Who Must Comply  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF81 | - **What the limit is.** A cliff, tested on the issuer, never on the customer. At the limit or below, the relief for 2027 sales applies. One euro above, it does not, for any 2027 sale. | Exact retained currentLF81, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF82 | - **What counts.** Total turnover under § 19(2) UStG: supplies within the scope of German VAT (steuerbare Umsätze), counted on a receipts basis, less certain exempt supplies, without sales of fixed assets. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF83 | - **Same number, other rules.** The same amount appears in § 141 AO (bookkeeping duty) and § 20 UStG (VAT on receipts). They are separate tests. | Exact retained currentLF83, Who Must Comply  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF92 | - **What the limit is.** The total of ONE invoice, VAT included. "Not more than": an invoice of exactly the amount in the table is still a small invoice. | Exact retained currentLF92, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF93 | - **The whole invoice counts.** If one invoice covers several supplies, only its total matters. Above the limit an e-invoice is due, even if the part that falls under the e-invoice duty is below it, for example because exempt or non-taxable items are billed on the same invoice (decree section 14.6(4)). | Exact retained currentLF93, Who Must Comply  |

Issuing an e-invoice is allowed too, and needs no consent when the customer is a German business. | Exact retained currentLF114, Who Must Comply R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF116 | - **Leaving the scheme.** A Kleinunternehmer who moves to normal taxation must issue e-invoices from that moment, under the general conditions (questions and answers, no. 4). | Exact retained currentLF116, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF118 | **Federal public contracts (ERechV)** | Exact retained currentLF118, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF120 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact retained currentLF76, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF122 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__3.html \| | Exact retained currentLF122, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF123 | \| No duty to invoice electronically for an invoice issued after a direct award (Direktauftrag) up to \| EUR 1,000 \| § 3(3) no. 1 ERechV: "die nach Erfüllung eines Direktauftrags bis zu einem Betrag von 1 000 Euro gestellt werden" \| | Exact retained currentLF123, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF125 | - **Other ERechV exceptions:** invoice data that must be kept secret (§ 8), the foreign service and other purchases abroad (§ 9), and Organleihe cases (§ 3(3) no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF125, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF126 | - **The ERechV does not say** whether its amount is net or gross. | Exact retained currentLF126, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF127 | - **Two sets of rules, side by side.** The ministry says there is no ranking between the UStG and the ERechV: each applies in its own field, and one can in practice narrow a choice the other leaves open (questions and answers, no. 4a). An invoice to a public body falls under the VAT e-invoice rules only when the body acts as a business. | Exact retained currentLF127, Who Must Comply  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF131 | All from the ministry's letter of 15 October 2025, decree section 14.1(4) and (5): https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | Exact retained currentLF131, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF133 | - **The duty.** "Inländische Unternehmer müssen die technischen Voraussetzungen zum Empfang einer E-Rechnung schaffen." This also applies to a recipient under § 19 UStG. | Exact retained currentLF133, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF134 | - **No consent.** Issuing an e-invoice to another German business does not need the recipient's consent. | Exact retained currentLF134, Receiving e-invoices R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF135 | - **E-mail.** E-invoices may come by e-mail. The letter says: "Bei einem Empfang mittels E-Mail ist kein gesondertes E-Mail-Postfach nur für den Empfang von E-Rechnungen erforderlich." The ministry's questions and answers page goes one step further and says an e-mail inbox is already enough to receive (nos. 8 and 12). | Exact retained currentLF135, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF136 | - **Refusing does not help.** A business that cannot receive an e-invoice, or refuses it, has no right to an other invoice instead. The issuer's VAT duties then count as met if it issued an e-invoice and can show that it tried to send it properly, for example with a send log. | Exact retained currentLF136, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF137 | - **Receiving is not processing.** The recipient need not process the data electronically beyond what storage demands (questions and answers, no. 9). | Exact retained currentLF137, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF138 | - **Reading the file.** An XRechnung has no picture part; a viewer is needed. The tax administration offers one for XRechnung and ZUGFeRD files at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung and calls it a non-binding service. | Exact retained currentLF138, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF139 | - **Associations.** An association must be able to receive e-invoices so far as it acts as a business. For supplies to its non-business side it need neither receive nor issue e-invoices (questions and answers, no. 5). | Exact retained currentLF139, Receiving e-invoices  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF143 | \| Invoice \| Consent of the recipient \| | Exact retained currentLF143, When consent is needed R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF149 | \| E-invoice where there is no e-invoice duty: consumers, exempt supplies under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, a supplier or customer abroad, a legal person that is not a business \| Needed \| | Exact retained currentLF149, When consent is needed  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF151 | - **Form of consent.** None. It can be implied, for example by accepting the invoice without objection, given in general terms of business, or given afterwards (decree section 14.1(7)). | Exact retained currentLF151, When consent is needed R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF159 | \| 2025 or 2026 \| A paper invoice. Or, with the recipient's consent, an invoice in another electronic format \| 31 December 2026 \| Every issuer. An e-invoice is due wherever § 14(2) sentence 2 no. 1 UStG demands one \| Not applicable \| The lasting exceptions stay: small invoices, tickets, Kleinunternehmer, exempt supplies under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, non-German parties \| | Exact retained currentLF162, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF164 | - **Two dates in each rule.** The statute ties each relief to when the supply was made AND to the day by which the invoice is sent. Read literally, an invoice sent in January 2027 for a sale made in December 2026 fits neither the first row (sent too late) nor the second (sale made too early). The pages read for this Guide do not settle that case. The ministry's questions and answers page (no. 11) describes the first relief the same way, as a period in which the invoice is issued, from 1 January 2025 to 31 December 2026. Invoice 2026 sales before the year ends, or refer. | Exact retained currentLF164, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) R39: selected own-default/transition method supplies the exact ordinary late-invoice fixture; actual prior advance/standing exemptions remain separate. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF165 | - **Issuing only.** § 27(38) UStG relieves issuers. There is no relief for receiving. | Exact retained currentLF165, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF166 | - **The issuer decides.** The choice to use the relief lies with the issuer. The ministry adds that the rules leave a recipient free to pick a supplier that, for now, still issues paper invoices (questions and answers, no. 11). | Exact retained currentLF166, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF167 | - **An other invoice still works while it is allowed.** As long as an other invoice may be issued, it remains a proper invoice for the input tax deduction (questions and answers, no. 2). | Exact retained currentLF167, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF168 | - **Supplies before 2025** follow the old rules, even if invoiced later (questions and answers, no. 2). | Exact retained currentLF168, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF169 | - **Payments on account.** Invoices for advance or part payments issued before the cut-off date need not be e-invoices, even if supply and payment come later (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF169, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF170 | - **Corrections during the transition** may still be made without an e-invoice (decree section 14.11(1); questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF170, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF171 | - **After the transition an agreed format stays possible.** § 14(1) sentence 6 no. 2 UStG allows a structured format agreed between the parties, if the required data can be extracted correctly and completely into a format that meets the European standard or works with it. The ministry names EDI procedures such as EDIFACT as examples. | Exact retained currentLF171, Transitional Rules (2025-2027)  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF199 | - **Which allowed format is used** is a civil law question between the parties (decree section 14.1(12)). | Exact retained currentLF199, Accepted Formats  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF200 | - **Syntax names.** The ministry says only that the standard prescribes XML. KoSIT's specification names the same two syntaxes, UBL and the UN/CEFACT Cross Industry Invoice (CII). | Exact retained currentLF200, Accepted Formats  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF201 | - **Hybrid files: the XML leads.** Before 2025 the picture part led. Now the structured part is the leading part, and if the two differ the structured data decide (decree section 14.4(3); questions and answers, no. 12a). Input tax can be deducted only from the structured part. A picture part with different invoice details can count as a further (other) invoice, for which § 14c UStG must be checked: that section makes a person owe tax that is shown wrongly or without the right to show it (decree section 14c.1(4a)). Small technical differences, such as a shortened description or rounding differences, are not objected to. | Exact retained currentLF201, Accepted Formats  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF202 | - **Everything the VAT law demands must be in the structured part.** All details under §§ 14 and 14a UStG belong there. A mere reference in the data to an attachment that holds them in unstructured form is not enough. A link to an outside target is not enough. Extra detail, such as a timesheet as a PDF, may sit in an attachment inside the e-invoice (decree section 14.5(1) and (15)). | Exact retained currentLF202, Accepted Formats  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF292 | The ERechV demands a federal administration portal and a user account set up before the first invoice (§ 4(3) ERechV, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html). A formally faulty invoice is rejected automatically and the sender is told. An invoice that cannot be matched to a user account is rejected without notice (§ 4(4) ERechV). The channels below are from the legacy Guide and from the federal e-invoicing information site named in the ministry's questions and answers page (e-rechnung-bund.de, run by the procurement office of the interior ministry). That site is not an allowed host for this Guide, so it is not linked, and the portal address in the legacy Guide was removed. | CurrentfederalrouteLF292–300. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF391 | - **Tax amount rounding:** the format has no tax amount per line. The tax is worked out once per VAT breakdown group (BG-23), that is per tax category and rate: the group's taxable amount (BT-116) times its rate (BT-119), divided by 100, rounded to two decimal places (rule BR-CO-17). The legacy Guide's tax amount per line was wrong | Exact retained currentLF391, Rounding  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF392 | - **Category totals:** for each tax category and each rate within it, the taxable amount (BT-116) must equal the sum of the line net amounts (BT-131) with that category and rate, plus document level charges, less document level allowances with that category and rate (rule BR-S-8 for category S) | Exact retained currentLF392, Rounding  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF393 | - **Validation tolerance:** the legacy Guide stated a one-cent tolerance per tax subtotal group. No allowed page prints it, so the amount was removed | Exact retained currentLF393, Rounding  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF394 | - **Rounding mode:** KoSIT's specification says only "gerundet auf zwei Dezimalstellen" and names no rounding mode. The legacy Guide's line on banker's rounding is not in it and was removed | Exact retained currentLF394, Rounding  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF395 | - **What the ministry says:** rounding differences between the picture part and the structured part of a hybrid invoice are not objected to (decree section 14c.1(4a)). | Exact retained currentLF395, Rounding  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF399 | Carried over from the legacy Guide (data format, not checked on an official page), except where a law or KoSIT's specification is named. | CurrentmultirateLF401–405. ReducedrateusesSnotAA; Zforbidsreason, E/AE/G/Orequireit; oldblanketallzero-taxreasoncorrected.  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF401 | - **Separate tax subtotal group per rate:** each VAT rate requires a separate tax subtotal group (BG-23). The law demands the net amount broken down by tax rate and by exemption (§ 14(4) no. 7 UStG) | Exact retained currentLF401, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF402 | - **Tax category codes:** KoSIT's XRechnung specification allows only S (Standard rate), Z (Zero rated goods), E (Exempt from tax), AE (VAT Reverse Charge), K (intra-Community supply), G (export, tax not charged), O (outside scope of tax), and L and M for the Canary Islands and for Ceuta and Melilla. The legacy Guide's code AA for the reduced rate is not in that list. A reduced-rate line carries S with its own rate: rule BR-S-5 asks only that the rate under S be greater than zero | CurrentmultirateLF401–405. ReducedrateusesSnotAA; Zforbidsreason, E/AE/G/Orequireit; oldblanketallzero-taxreasoncorrected.  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF403 | - **Reverse charge category code:** for reverse charge (§ 13b UStG), use category code AE with a rate of zero. The invoice must carry the words "Steuerschuldnerschaft des Leistungsempfängers", and no tax is shown (§ 14a(5) UStG). The e-invoice duty applies to reverse-charge supplies between two German businesses (decree section 14.1(4)) | Exact retained currentLF403, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF404 | - **Tax exemption reason population:** the tax exemption reason (BT-120/BT-121) must be given for the categories E, AE, G and O (rules BR-E-10, BR-AE-10, BR-G-10, BR-O-10) and must NOT be given for the zero rated category Z (rule BR-Z-10). The law demands a note that an exemption applies (§ 14(4) no. 8 UStG) | Exact retained currentLF404, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF405 | - **Partly exempt invoices.** If only part of what is billed falls under the e-invoice duty, for example taxable and § 4 nos. 8 to 29 exempt items together, the whole invoice must be an e-invoice (decree section 14.1(4)) | Exact retained currentLF405, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF409 | German VAT law (§ 14(4) UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html) lists the content of an invoice. In an e-invoice all of it must be in the structured data: | Exact retained currentLF409, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF411 | 1. Full name and full address of the supplier and of the customer | Exact retained currentLF411, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF412 | 2. The supplier's tax number (Steuernummer) or VAT identification number (USt-IdNr.) | Exact retained currentLF412, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF413 | 3. Issue date | Exact retained currentLF413, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF414 | 4. A sequential invoice number, given once | Exact retained currentLF414, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF415 | 5. Quantity and kind (usual trade description) of the goods, or scope and kind of the service | Exact retained currentLF415, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF416 | 6. Time of the supply; for payments on account, the time of receipt if it is fixed and differs from the issue date | Exact retained currentLF416, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF417 | 7. The net amount broken down by tax rate and by exemption, and any reduction agreed in advance that is not yet in the price | Exact retained currentLF417, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF418 | 8. The tax rate and the tax amount, or a note that an exemption applies | Exact retained currentLF418, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF419 | 9. In the cases of § 14b(1) sentence 5 UStG, a note that the recipient must keep the invoice | Exact retained currentLF419, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF420 | 10. The word "Gutschrift" where the customer issues the invoice (self-billing) | Exact retained currentLF420, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF422 | - **Date of supply.** A phrase such as "Leistungsdatum entspricht Rechnungsdatum" is enough only on an other invoice. In an e-invoice the date must be in the structured field. A reference to a delivery note works only for other invoices (decree section 14.5(16)). The calendar month may be given as the time of supply, so several supplies of one month can go on one collective e-invoice (§ 31(4) UStDV; questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF422, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF423 | - **Cash discount.** A note such as the agreed discount terms is enough; the discount need not be shown as an amount (decree section 14.5(19)). | Exact retained currentLF423, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF424 | - **Small invoices, tickets and Kleinunternehmer invoices** need no sequential number (decree section 14.5(14)). | Exact retained currentLF424, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF428 | \| Requirement \| Detail \| | Exact retained currentLF428, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF430 | \| Retention period \| 8 years for a copy of every invoice issued and for every invoice received (§ 14b(1) UStG). The period starts at the end of the calendar year in which the invoice was issued. § 147(3) AO stays untouched: under it the period does not end while the documents still matter for a tax whose assessment period is open. Start of the eight-year period: it applies to every invoice whose old retention period had not yet run out on 31 December 2024; for banks, insurers and securities institutions only to invoices whose old period had not run out on 1 January 2026 (§ 27(40) UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html) \| | Exact retained currentLF430, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF431 | \| Two-year duty for private recipients \| A consumer, or a business buying for its private side, who receives a taxable property-related service must keep the invoice, a payment record or other proof for two years (§ 14b(1) sentence 5 UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF431, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF432 | \| Format \| Keep the invoice in the format in which it was received (decree section 14b.1(1); GoBD paragraph 131). For an e-invoice, at least the structured part must be kept so that it is unchanged and in its original form, and the tax office must be able to evaluate it by machine (decree section 14b.1(5)) \| | Exact retained currentLF432, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF433 | \| GoBD compliance \| The GoBD (ministry letter of 28 November 2019, changed on 11 March 2024 and 14 July 2025) apply for income tax and bookkeeping purposes. For VAT alone, the ministry says that storing e-invoices outside a GoBD-compliant system is as a rule ("regelmäßig") no breach of § 14b(1) UStG (decree section 14b.1(1)) \| | Exact retained currentLF433, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF434 | \| Hybrid invoices (ZUGFeRD) \| It is enough to keep the structured part. The human-readable part must be kept only if it holds extra or different information that matters for tax, for example booking notes or qualified electronic signatures (GoBD paragraphs 119 and 131). The XML must not be lost through a format change, for example to TIFF (GoBD paragraph 125) \| | Exact retained currentLF434, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF435 | \| Outgoing invoices \| With an invoicing program, no picture copy of the outgoing invoice (a PDF, or the PDF part of a hybrid file) needs to be stored, if an identical copy can be produced on request at any time (GoBD paragraph 76) \| | Exact retained currentLF435, Section 8: Archiving Requirements R42: reconstruction waiver requires actual highest-evaluability/full-content/history/layout/documentation/immutability gates. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF436 | \| Content, not picture \| Vouchers received as structured data need to match in content only, not in appearance (GoBD paragraph 118) \| | Exact retained currentLF436, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF437 | \| Immutability \| Authenticity of origin, integrity of content and legibility must be secured for the whole period (§ 14b(1) sentence 2 with § 14(3) UStG). For an e-invoice, legible means machine-readable; a human-readable copy is not required (decree section 14.4(3)). A qualified electronic signature, if used, must be kept too (decree section 14b.1(6)) \| | Exact retained currentLF437, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF438 | \| Verfahrensdokumentation \| The legacy Guide says written procedural documentation of the e-invoice process (receipt, processing, archiving) is mandatory. That comes from the GoBD main letter of 28 November 2019, which is not among the pages read for this Guide. Carried over, not checked \| | Exact retained currentLF438, Section 8: Archiving Requirements R43: actual135default/conditionedexception and150–155documentation/qualification now supplied; mandatory originalXMLretained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF439 | \| Storage location \| In Germany. Electronic storage elsewhere in the EU is allowed if full online access, download and use of the data are secured; the tax office must then be told the place (§ 14b(2) and (4) UStG). Outside the EU only under § 146(2b) AO, which needs the tax office's approval on application (§ 14b(5) UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF439, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF440 | \| Audit access \| Three forms under § 147(6) AO: the tax office inspects the stored data and uses the system itself (Z1, direct access); it demands that the data be evaluated by machine as it specifies (Z2, indirect access); or it demands that the data be handed over in a machine-readable format (Z3, data handover, for example on a data carrier or through a data exchange platform). The legacy Guide still called Z3 a data carrier handover; the GoBD change of 11 March 2024 renamed it \| | Exact retained currentLF440, Section 8: Archiving Requirements  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF441 | \| Conversion \| Converting an incoming electronic document to another format is allowed only under the conditions of GoBD paragraph 135 (GoBD paragraph 131). Paragraph 135 itself is in the main letter, which was not read. The legacy Guide's summary: the original must also be kept and the conversion documented \| | Exact retained currentLF441, Section 8: Archiving Requirements R43: actual135default/conditionedexception and150–155documentation/qualification now supplied; mandatory originalXMLretained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF455 | \| Other invoice where an e-invoice was due: input VAT deduction \| The invoice is not a proper invoice under §§ 14 and 14a UStG, so in principle it gives no right to deduct input tax (decree section 15.2a(1)). | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF500 | - Verfahrensdokumentation must describe the e-invoice workflow from receipt to archiving (legacy statement, from the GoBD main letter, not checked) | Exact retained currentLF500, GoBD and Tax Audit R43: actual135default/conditionedexception and150–155documentation/qualification now supplied; mandatory originalXMLretained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF501 | - If the archive cannot be produced in an audit, the tax office can estimate (§ 162 AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html), and a delay payment can be set when data access is not granted: see Section 9 | Exact retained currentLF501, GoBD and Tax Audit  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF505 | All from the letter of 15 October 2025 (decree sections in brackets) at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 or from the questions and answers page at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/FAQ/e-rechnung.html | Exact retained currentLF505, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF507 | - **Self-billing (Gutschrift).** The e-invoice rules apply in the same way when the customer issues the invoice. If a self-billing document and an ordinary invoice cannot be combined in one e-invoice for technical reasons, separate e-invoices are issued (14.3(1) and (2)). | Exact retained currentLF507, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF508 | - **Special schemes.** The duty also covers supplies under the special rules of §§ 23a to 25c UStG (for example travel services and the margin scheme) and reverse-charge supplies under § 13b UStG between German businesses (14.1(4)). | Exact retained currentLF508, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF509 | - **Opted supplies.** A supply that is taxable only because of an option under § 9(1) UStG needs an e-invoice (14.1(4)). | Exact retained currentLF509, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF510 | - **Rent and other continuing contracts.** One e-invoice for the first part period is enough, if the contract is attached to it or the e-invoice otherwise makes clear that it is a standing invoice. When an invoice detail changes, for example a rent increase, a new e-invoice is due. A standing invoice that was properly issued as an other invoice needs no extra e-invoice as long as its details do not change (14.1(19), 14.5(17)). The invoice number may be a number from the contract, such as a flat or tenant number (14.5(12)). | Exact retained currentLF510, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF511 | - **Contracts as invoices.** A contract with all invoice details is an other invoice unless it meets the format rules. Where an e-invoice is due, an e-invoice must be issued in addition; the contract can sit in its attachment (14.1(17)). | Exact retained currentLF511, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF512 | - **Bank statements** that bill the bank's own service are invoices. They are e-invoices only if they meet the format and content rules (14.1(16)). | Exact retained currentLF512, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF513 | - **Inside one business.** Documents for movements between departments of the same business, or inside a VAT group (Organkreis), are internal vouchers, not invoices (14.1(1)). The e-invoice duty does not reach them. | Exact retained currentLF513, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF514 | - **Customer with a business and a private side.** For a legal person that buys for both sides, the e-invoice duty takes priority (14.1(8)). Where several persons buy jointly and businesses are among them, the invoice to that community must be an e-invoice once no transition relief applies (15.2a(3)). | Exact retained currentLF514, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled R41: full strict objective-evidence fallback now supplied, not automaticPDFcredit or blanketdenial. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF515 | - **Corrections.** Where an e-invoice was due, the correction must also be an e-invoice, using the invoice type for corrections. Sending the missing or wrong detail in another form is not enough (14.11(1)). Single line items may be corrected in a later e-invoice if it refers specifically and clearly to the first one (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF515, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF516 | - **Price changes are not corrections.** Cash discounts, rebates and bonuses paid later, and price cuts after a complaint that leave the supply itself unchanged, change the tax base under § 17 UStG; the invoice need not be corrected. A change in what was supplied, such as changed quantities on a building invoice, does need a correction, at least of the description (14.11(4), 17.1(3a)). | Exact retained currentLF516, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF517 | - **Building work.** For now it is enough if the structured part lists the single trades with their sums, and a human-readable attachment, clearly referred to in the structured part, gives the detailed breakdown (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF517, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF518 | - **Final invoices.** Part payments already received may be deducted in an unstructured attachment, if the structured part points to it expressly. The ministry says this can also be used after 31 December 2027 (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF518, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF519 | - **Fee notices of public bodies** that count as invoices follow the general rules, e-invoice duty included; one issued as an other invoice can be corrected by an e-invoice later (14.1(1)). | Exact retained currentLF519, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF523 | 1. Check whether an invoice is due at all. § 14(2) sentence 2 UStG demands one, within six months, for supplies to another business for its business, to a legal person that is not a business, and for taxable property-related work for anyone else. No duty for supplies exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html | Exact retained currentLF523, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF524 | 2. Check whether it must be an e-invoice. Yes, if supplier and customer are both established in Germany and the customer buys for its business (§ 14(2) sentence 2 no. 1 UStG). No, if one of them is established abroad, or the customer is a consumer or a legal person that is not a business. Same page as step 1. | Exact retained currentLF524, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF525 | 3. Check the lasting exceptions: small invoices (§ 33 UStDV), passenger tickets (§ 34 UStDV), invoices of a Kleinunternehmer (§ 34a UStDV). These may always be other invoices. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__33.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34a.html | Exact retained currentLF525, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF526 | 4. Check the transition relief in § 27(38) UStG: the year of the supply, the day the invoice is sent and, for 2027 sales, the issuer's total turnover of the previous calendar year. If the relief applies and the issuer wants to use it: paper, or with the recipient's consent another electronic format. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html | Exact retained currentLF526, The method, step by step R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF527 | 5. If an e-invoice is due or wanted, pick an allowed format: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD from version 2.0.1 without MINIMUM and BASIC-WL, another format that meets the European standard, or an agreed format that allows full extraction. Put every detail of § 14(4) and § 14a UStG into the structured part. Ministry letter of 15 October 2025: https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | Exact retained currentLF527, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF528 | 6. Validate the file, keep the validation report, and send it by a channel agreed with the customer. The customer's consent to the e-invoice is not needed. Keep proof of sending. Same letter as step 5. | Exact retained currentLF528, The method, step by step R40: no-consent summary follows actual duty/domestic33/34/34acategories; remaining voluntary cases require consent. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF529 | 7. On the receiving side: make sure e-invoices can be received, display the XML with a viewer (the tax administration offers one at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung ), and check the invoice details before deducting input tax under § 15 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__15.html | Exact retained currentLF529, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF530 | 8. Correct a faulty invoice under § 31(5) UStDV with a document that refers specifically and clearly to it. Where an e-invoice was due, the correction is an e-invoice too. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__31.html | Exact retained currentLF530, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF531 | 9. Store the structured part unchanged, in the format received, for the period in § 14b(1) UStG, and keep it machine-readable for the tax office. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html | Exact retained currentLF531, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF532 | 10. For a federal authority, follow the ERechV as well: XRechnung or an equal standard, Leitweg-ID, sign-up at the federal portal. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html | Exact retained currentLF532, The method, step by step  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF536 | - Are you and your customer both established in Germany (seat, management, or a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply), and does the customer buy for its business? | Exact retained currentLF536, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF537 | - When was the supply made, and when will the invoice be sent? The relief depends on both dates. | Exact retained currentLF537, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF538 | - What was your total turnover (§ 19(2) UStG) in the previous calendar year, and are you a Kleinunternehmer? | Exact retained currentLF538, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF539 | - What is the gross total of the invoice, and is any part of the supply exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG? | Exact retained currentLF539, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF540 | - What does your software produce and read today: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD (which version and profile), EDI, or plain PDF? Can you receive, display and store an XML file? | Exact retained currentLF540, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF541 | - Is the customer a public authority? If so, federal or state, and do you have its Leitweg-ID? | Exact retained currentLF541, Ask the client first  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF545 | - Invoices to state and local authorities: each federal state has its own rules, which this Guide does not cover. | Exact retained currentLF545, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF546 | - Technical set-up: field mapping, validator messages, Peppol access, portal sign-up. Refer to the software vendor and to the KoSIT and FeRD material. | Exact retained currentLF546, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF547 | - Whether a customer or supplier is "established" in Germany through a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply. Refer to a Steuerberater. | Exact retained currentLF547, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF548 | - Whether a supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, or taxable by option. | Exact retained currentLF548, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF549 | - A dispute over input tax where a paper or PDF invoice arrived although an e-invoice was due. | Exact retained currentLF549, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF550 | - Fine proceedings and anything that may be tax evasion. | Exact retained currentLF550, When to refuse or refer  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF551 | - Disputes between the parties over format or channel: the ministry treats these as civil law. | Exact retained currentLF551, When to refuse or refer  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF554 | - E-invoicing duties of other countries, and invoices under § 14(7) UStG that follow another member state's rules. | Exact retained currentLF554, When to refuse or refer  |
